# SS-9 Transaction-Indexed Transcript

**File:** `transcript-SS-9.md`
**Paper:** SS-9 — Conditional Derivation of Simplicial Alpha-Polytope Connectivity from CPP Lattice Geometry (working title)
**Role:** Tier 2 of the four-tier documentation discipline. Transaction-indexed pointer-map for every substantive transaction in SS-9's development. Each entry names the transaction and points to the artefact that holds its content. The transcript is a roadmap, not an archive — substance lives at the pointer targets.
**Companion files:** `reasoning-SS-9.md` (Tier 4 — verbatim Opus reasoning), `development-SS-9.md` (Tier 3 — curated vignettes). See `templates/operating_system.md` §11 (paper subfolder convention) and §15 reconciliation with §4.

---

## Conventions

**Transaction ID:** three-digit zero-padded, monotonically increasing, never reused (`001`, `002`, ...).
**Entry format:** `[ID] [date] — [one-line description] → [pointer or "see vignette N" or "see reasoning Session N"]`
**Pointer target:** a committed file, or a vignette ID in `development-SS-9.md`, or a session reference in `reasoning-SS-9.md`, or "(no artefact — decision recorded inline)."
**Append-only.** Transactions are never renumbered. If a transaction is later invalidated, record the invalidation as a new transaction pointing back to the invalidated ID.

### Session 4 — v0.3 graph-theoretic Lemma B$'$ restructuring (2 May 2026)

- `044` 2 May Session 4 — Session 4 bootup: cloned repo, read 26 April Session 3 handover end-to-end, read v0.2 working draft §3 Lemma B and §5 Theorem carefully, read SS-7 v1.3 §2.1 refined-C1 with facets (a)/(b)/(c), read founders_voice slip-plane intuition, read Table 1 residual fingerprint, scanned reasoning-SS-9.md Session 1–3 → see reasoning Session 4 "Bootup and orientation"
- `045` 2 May Session 4 — Bootup-staleness flag: public `raw.githubusercontent.com/.../bootup.md` URL served CDN-cached content dated 8 April 2026 (missing patches 0022 and 0049); confirmed via `git log` in cloned repo that both Step 0 (clone-first) and §3.5 (Four-Tier Discipline) are present on `main`; raised as operational note for Thomas, no patch needed → see reasoning Session 4 "Bootup-staleness operational note"
- `046` 2 May Session 4 — Diagnosis: v0.2 forward-direction supporting-hyperplane gap is substantive, not just "needs sharpening." The contact face $F_{ij}$ has nucleon-position vertices (not centroid vertices), so $F_{ij}$ does not directly bound $H = \text{conv}(c_i)$. Rigid packing forbids other *alphas* from intersecting $\overline{c_i c_j}$ but does not forbid the convex hull of other *centroids* from crossing it → see reasoning Session 4 "Diagnosis of the v0.2 forward-direction gap"; see vignette 5
- `047` 2 May Session 4 — Diagnosis: refined-C1 dissolves the strict-4-face/degree-5 inconsistency but does NOT directly close the supporting-hyperplane gap. The two are orthogonal concerns: the supporting-hyperplane gap would persist even at $N_\alpha = 4$ where strict-C1 is consistent. Refined-C1's load-bearing role for OPEN-SS-24 closure must come from elsewhere → see reasoning Session 4 "Refined-C1 doesn't directly close the gap"; see vignette 5
- `048` 2 May Session 4 — Three options surfaced: (A) FvdW-realization restructuring with new C7 hypothesis + Steinitz black-box; (B) attempt rigorous supporting-hyperplane construction with strengthened C6 or careful local-geometry argument; (C) pause/discuss before committing. Recommendation to Thomas: option (A) → see reasoning Session 4 "Three options for v0.3"; see vignette 5
- `049` 2 May Session 4 — Thomas selected option (A); requested bootup-staleness decision (rewrite or register OPEN-ORG); Opus confirmed bootup already correct on main, no patch needed → see reasoning Session 4 "Thomas's selection"
- `050` 2 May Session 4 — v0.3 framing locked: (i) add C7 (contact-graph planarity) as new paper-level hypothesis at C5/C6 inheritance tier; (ii) replace v0.2 Lemma B with graph-theoretic Lemma B$'$ via C7 + Lemma C + Euler + Steinitz (3-connectedness from triangulation-of-$S^2$ standard result); (iii) FvdW classification at listed $N_\alpha$ for Theorem clause (iv); (iv) refined-C1 facet (b) load-bearing in clause (iv) (existence of geometric realization at $N_\alpha \geq 7$ where strict-C1 cannot host degree-5) → see reasoning Session 4 "v0.3 framing decisions"; see vignette 5

## Forward-looking transactions (next session)

**v0.3 → v0.1 paper-text transition.** With the v0.3 conditional-theorem structure tight (Lemma A, Lemma B$'$ via C7+Steinitz, Lemma C, Theorem with FvdW realization at listed $N_\alpha$ via facet (b)), the next natural deliverable is the SS-9 v0.1 paper text at `series_strong/papers/SS-9/SS-9_simplicial_alpha_polytope_connectivity.tex`. The conversion is mostly mechanical: convert markdown to LaTeX, add the SS-7 §2.1 figure pattern, write the abstract / introduction / discussion sections, register at OSF. Before drafting, decide whether C4 itself needs refinement under the C1$'$+C5+C6+C7 stack (Session 3 deliberately left C4 unrefined; SS-9 is the right place to revisit).

**Alternative: Phase 4 attempts on any of OPEN-SS-29/30/31/32/33.** If the v0.3 conditional-theorem structure feels solid enough to ship as outline, future sessions could pivot to programme-level closure attempts on the conditionals. Most leverage is plausibly OPEN-SS-33 (C7 first-principles closure), since it is the new hypothesis introduced in v0.3 and may close cheaply via the cluster-shell-topology argument sketched in v0.3 §9.

**Each substantive turn in the next session appends a transaction here, a vignette to `development-SS-9.md` if the transaction is paper-relevant, and Opus reasoning to `reasoning-SS-9.md`.** The four-tier discipline runs continuously across the development arc.

- `228` 2 May Session 10 — Diagonal HO matrix elements: $\langle n,l|r^4|n,l\rangle = (\hbar/m_n\omega)^2 \cdot f(N,l)$ where $f(N,l) = (N+3/2)^2 + 2n(n+l+3/2) + l + 3/2$, $N = 2n+l$. - `267` 4 May Session 12 — Force-balance equation for screened internal contact: $V_{K_3}'(R^*) + f_{\rm eff}^2 \cdot V_{\rm Coul}'(R^*) = 0$. - `273` 4 May Session 12 — Forward pointer: U-shape pattern structurally similar to **SS-7 OPEN-SS-32** (Cluster-level collective oblate-deformation mode, regime B at $N_\alpha \in \{7, 8, 9, 10\}$ with $+0.55 B_{\rm pair}$ excess in SS-7 binding-energy fit). - `275` 4 May Session 12 — Decoupling Theorem proof: at $A = 56$, CPP gives $\hbar\omega = 13$ MeV, $V_{\rm SO} = 1.17$ MeV, ratio $0.090$; empirical $\hbar\omega = 10.7$ MeV, $V_{\rm SO} \sim 1.5$ MeV, ratio $0.140$. ### Session 14 Phase 3B-A — Minimal fixed-dim belt-subspace projection RULED OUT (5 May 2026)

- `318` 5 May Session 14 Phase 3B-A — Strategy: simplest tractable IRREP-selective realization specified in Phase 3A §6. Project full Hessian eigenmodes onto a minimal belt subspace (3D basis: A$_1$ in-plane radial breathing monopole + 2D E$_2$ quadrupole cos/sin patterns) at each axial polytope; weight per-mode zero-point variance by $f_k^{\rm belt}$. Test three sharply constrained quantitative targets registered at Session 13 close → `series_strong/papers/SS-9/sketches/SS-9_OPEN-SS-32_Ushape_unification_phase3b_a.md` §1
- `319` 5 May Session 14 Phase 3B-A — Inertia classification: compute $I_{ab} = \sum_i (\delta_{ab} r_i^2 - r_{i,a} r_{i,b})$ about COM; diagonalise. Three regimes: DEGENERATE (all eigenvalues equal within $10^{-3}$ relative tolerance) → T$_d$, O$_h$, I$_h$, no preferred axis, dim(B) = 0 by symmetry; PROLATE (smallest unique) → bipyramids; OBLATE (largest unique) → discus shapes. Implements Reading A's structural commitment "no belt-IRREP at fully-symmetric polytopes" → `SS-9_OPEN-SS-32_Ushape_unification_phase3b_a.md` §2.1
- `320` 5 May Session 14 Phase 3B-A — Belt basis construction: cylindrical coords $(\rho_i, \varphi_i, z_i)$ in principal-axis frame. Three raw belt basis vectors (length 3$N$ each): $b^{(0)}_i = \hat \rho_i$ (monopole), $b^{(c)}_i = \cos(2\varphi_i) \hat\rho_i$, $b^{(s)}_i = \sin(2\varphi_i) \hat\rho_i$ (E$_2$ quadrupole). Project out 6-dim rigid-body subspace (3 translations + 3 rotations about COM); Gram-Schmidt orthonormalise survivors → orthonormal belt basis $\{\hat e^a\}$ with $\dim(B) \le 3$ → `SS-9_OPEN-SS-32_Ushape_unification_phase3b_a.md` §2.2
- `321` 5 May Session 14 Phase 3B-A — Belt fraction per mode $f_k^{\rm belt} = \sum_a |\langle \hat e^a | v_k \rangle|^2 \in [0,1]$. Belt-projected variance: $\langle (\delta r)^2\rangle_{\rm belt} = (1/|E|) \sum_k f_k^{\rm belt} \cdot C_k^{\rm edge}$. In the limit $f_k^{\rm belt} = 1$ ∀$k$, recovers Phase 3A full result; in the limit $f_k^{\rm belt} = 0$ ∀$k$ (DEGEN polytopes), exactly zero → `SS-9_OPEN-SS-32_Ushape_unification_phase3b_a.md` §2.3
- `322` 5 May Session 14 Phase 3B-A — Eight-row table of belt-projected softening: T$_d$ DEGEN dim(B)=0 $\delta_{\rm belt}=0\%$; D$_{3h}$ N=5 PROLATE dim(B)=3 $\delta_{\rm belt}=-33.1\%$; O$_h$ DEGEN dim(B)=0 $\delta_{\rm belt}=0\%$; D$_{5h}$ N=7 OBLATE dim(B)=3 $-17.2\%$; D$_{2d}$ N=8 PROLATE dim(B)=2 $-9.4\%$; D$_{3h}$ N=9 OBLATE dim(B)=3 $-11.4\%$; D$_{4d}$ N=10 PROLATE dim(B)=3 $-8.2\%$; I$_h$ DEGEN dim(B)=0 $\delta_{\rm belt}=0\%$. dim(B)=2 at N=8 from D$_{2d}$ symmetry-induced rank reduction (belt cos/sin merged with monopole under C$_2$) → `SS-9_OPEN-SS-32_Ushape_unification_phase3b_a.md` §3.1

### Session 15 Phase 3B-B — Full C$_n$ IRREP decomposition RULED OUT; n-vs-N structural argument FORMALLY CLOSES R2 (5 May 2026)

- `335` 5 May Session 15 Phase 3B-B — Strategy: natural single-session realization of "full character-theory IRREP decomposition" with belt-IRREP dimension scaling, per Session 14 Phase 3B-A close handover. Project full Hessian eigenmodes onto belt-IRREP subspaces using each axial polytope's C$_n$ proper-rotation subgroup. Three natural belt-IRREP variants tested simultaneously: B-B1 "all $m \neq 0$" (broadest, every axially-anisotropic mode); B-B2 "$m = 2$ only" (oblate-quadrupole IRREP, SS-7 OPEN-SS-32 hypothesis target); B-B3 "$m \neq 0$ AND in-plane radial" (dimension-scaling generalization of Phase 3B-A's fixed 3-dim belt-radial subspace). DEGENERATE polytopes (T$_d$, O$_h$, I$_h$) get $\dim(\text{belt}) = 0$ by symmetry, identical to Phase 3B-A → `series_strong/papers/SS-9/sketches/SS-9_OPEN-SS-32_Ushape_unification_phase3b_b.md` §1
- `336` 5 May Session 15 Phase 3B-B — C$_n$ proper-rotation subgroup as upper bound on full-point-group: C$_n$ decomposition is coarser than the full point group D$_{nh}$/D$_{nd}$ which include reflections and improper rotations; restricting to a finer decomposition can only reduce variance. So C$_n$ overshoot ⇒ full-point-group cannot rescue (overshoots more); C$_n$ undershoot ⇒ full-point-group undershoots more. Bracketing property makes C$_n$ the right single-session test → `SS-9_OPEN-SS-32_Ushape_unification_phase3b_b.md` §1
- `337` 5 May Session 15 Phase 3B-B — Cyclic-symmetry detection: principal axis from Phase 3B-A's inertia classification; try $n \in \{2, 3, 4, 5\}$ and select largest $n$ for which rotation by $2\pi/n$ about the axis maps the polytope vertex set to itself within numerical tolerance $0.05 \cdot R_\alpha$. Detected $n$: $N=5 \to n=3$ (D$_{3h}$, C$_3$); $N=7 \to n=5$ (D$_{5h}$, C$_5$); $N=8 \to n=2$ (D$_{2d}$, C$_2$ proper only — full D$_{2d}$ has S$_4$ improper); $N=9 \to n=3$ (D$_{3h}$, C$_3$); $N=10 \to n=4$ (D$_{4d}$, C$_4$ proper only) → `SS-9_OPEN-SS-32_Ushape_unification_phase3b_b.md` §2.1
- `338` 5 May Session 15 Phase 3B-B — Rotation operator on displacement space: under C$_n$ rotation, vertex $i$ maps to vertex $\pi(i)$ via induced permutation $\pi$. The 3$N$-dim displacement-space rotation operator $R_n$ is a tensor product of vertex permutation and 3D rotation: $R_n[3\pi(i)\!:\!3\pi(i)+3,\, 3i\!:\!3i+3] = R_{3D}$ where $R_{3D}$ is the 3D rotation matrix by $2\pi/n$ about the principal axis (zero elsewhere). Rodrigues formula used for $R_{3D}$ → `SS-9_OPEN-SS-32_Ushape_unification_phase3b_b.md` §2.2
- `339` 5 May Session 15 Phase 3B-B — Real-valued IRREP projectors: $P_m = (1/n) \sum_{j=0}^{n-1} \cos(2\pi m j / n) R_n^j$ for $m \in \{0, 1, \ldots, n-1\}$. Properties: $\sum_m P_m = I$ (completeness, verified by Σ trace $P_m = 3N$ for all polytopes); $P_m = P_{n-m}$ (cosine symmetry, m and n-m paired into degenerate doublet partners); for $n=3$: $P_2 \equiv P_1$; for $n=2$: $P_2 \equiv P_0$ (not separate IRREP). Cosine projection captures same content as orthogonal complex-exponential decomposition via real combinations → `SS-9_OPEN-SS-32_Ushape_unification_phase3b_b.md` §2.3
- `340` 5 May Session 15 Phase 3B-B — Belt-IRREP fractions per mode: B-B1 $f_k^{\rm B-B1} = ||(I - P_0) v_k||^2 = 1 - ||P_0 v_k||^2$ (independent of cosine-projector pairing redundancies); B-B2 $f_k^{\rm B-B2} = ||P_2 v_k||^2$ computed only when $n \geq 3$; B-B3 $f_k^{\rm B-B3} = ||P_{\rm rad} \cdot (I - P_0) v_k||^2$ where $P_{\rm rad}$ is the per-vertex in-plane radial-direction projector (Phase 3B-A construction). Variance weighting by Phase 3B-A scheme → `SS-9_OPEN-SS-32_Ushape_unification_phase3b_b.md` §2.4–§2.5
- `341` 5 May Session 15 Phase 3B-B — Eight-row results table at $\sigma_{K3} = 1.68$ fm: $N=4$ T$_d$ DEGEN $-d_{\rm BB1}=0$; $N=5$ D$_{3h}$ $n=3$ $-d_{\rm BB1}=50.09$, $-d_{\rm BB2}=12.52$, $-d_{\rm BB3}=13.94$; $N=6$ O$_h$ DEGEN $0$; $N=7$ D$_{5h}$ $n=5$ $68.24, 7.39, 20.71$; $N=8$ D$_{2d}$ $n=2$ $38.30, 0.00, 10.97$; $N=9$ D$_{3h}$ $n=3$ $54.06, 13.51, 16.08$; $N=10$ D$_{4d}$ $n=4$ $62.01, 19.23, 14.98$; $N=12$ I$_h$ DEGEN $0$. Empirical: $N=4: +11.51$, $N=5: -12.16$, $N=6: -21.27$, $N=7: -29.50$, $N=8: -31.81$, $N=9: -33.14$, $N=10: -33.58$, $N=12: +1.41$ → `SS-9_OPEN-SS-32_Ushape_unification_phase3b_b.md` §3.1
- `342` 5 May Session 15 Phase 3B-B — Sanity checks all pass: Phase 3A reproduction exact to 3 decimals across all 8 polytopes; Σ trace $P_m = 3N$ verified ($5+5+5=15$ at $N=5$; $5+5+3+3+5=21$ at $N=7$; $12+12=24$ at $N=8$; $9+9+9=27$ at $N=9$; $8+8+6+8=30$ at $N=10$); DEGENERATE polytopes get $f_{\rm belt} = 0$ by symmetry; cyclic-symmetry detection correct; Σ $f_k^{\rm B-B1}$ matches expected mode count by IRREP per polytope → `SS-9_OPEN-SS-32_Ushape_unification_phase3b_b.md` §3.1
- `343` 5 May Session 15 Phase 3B-B — DECISIVE FINDING (n-vs-N structural obstacle): empirical magnitude monotonically increasing in $N$ across J-solid range — $|\delta_{\rm emp}| = 12.16, 29.50, 31.81, 33.14, 33.58\%$ for $N = 5, 7, 8, 9, 10$. But cyclic symmetry order $n$ non-monotonic in $N$: $n = 3, 5, 2, 3, 4$ for same $N$. Full point group orders also non-monotonic: $|G| = 12, 20, 8, 12, 16$. Any belt-IRREP-projection mechanism's variance content depends on $n$ (or $|G|$), so no function of group-theoretic structure can produce a monotonic-in-$N$ pattern when $n$ is non-monotonic in $N$ → `SS-9_OPEN-SS-32_Ushape_unification_phase3b_b.md` §3.2 / §4.1
- `344` 5 May Session 15 Phase 3B-B — n-vs-N argument is class-level: rules out the entire family of belt-IRREP-projection mechanisms within K$_3$-Gaussian-Hessian framework, not just the three Phase 3B-B variants. Leading anharmonic Taylor coefficient $-(B_{\rm pair}/8) < 0$. In harmonic GS, $\langle \xi^4 \rangle_0 = 3 \langle \xi^2 \rangle_0^2 > 0$ (Wick). First-order PT shift $\Delta E^{(1)}_{\rm anharm} = -(3 B_{\rm pair}/8) \langle \xi^2 \rangle_0^2 < 0$ (more binding). Empirical J-solid range needs $\Delta E > 0$ (cluster wants to grow → less binding than canonical K$_3$). Signs opposite. F1 (sign) fails before computation → `SS-9_OPEN-SS-32_Ushape_phase4_anharmonic_K3_xi4.md` §2.1–§2.2

- `364` 5 May Session 16 Phase 4 — All-orders Gaussian average extension: $\langle V_{\rm pair} \rangle_{\rm HOgs} = -B_{\rm pair} (1+s)^{-1/2}$ for harmonic-GS-trial Gaussian wavefunction with per-edge variance $s$. All-orders shift $\Delta E_{\rm anharm}^{\rm all\text{-}orders} = -B_{\rm pair} \sum_{\rm edges} f(s_{ab})$ with $f(s) \equiv (1+s)^{-1/2} - 1 + s/2$. Numerical results: factor $\sim 0.59$ reduction from leading $\xi^4$ estimate (polytope-independent because $\langle s \rangle$ near-constant); sign preserved across all 8 polytopes → `SS-9_OPEN-SS-32_Ushape_phase4_anharmonic_K3_xi4.md` §2.3 / §3.3
- `365` 5 May Session 16 Phase 4 — DECISIVE FINDING — sign theorem: $f(s) \equiv (1+s)^{-1/2} - 1 + s/2$ is strictly positive for all $s > 0$. Proof: $f(0) = 0$; $f'(s) = (1/2)[1 - (1+s)^{-3/2}] > 0$ for $s > 0$ since $(1+s)^{-3/2} < 1$; therefore $f$ strictly increasing on $(0, \infty)$ from $f(0) = 0$, hence $f(s) > 0$ for $s > 0$. Therefore $\Delta E_{\rm anharm}^{\rm all\text{-}orders} = -B_{\rm pair} f(s) < 0$ universally → `SS-9_OPEN-SS-32_Ushape_phase4_anharmonic_K3_xi4.md` §2.4

True cluster ground state in full Gaussian potential is *more* bound than harmonic estimate, never less. No reordering, truncation, or resummation can flip this → `SS-9_OPEN-SS-32_Ushape_phase4_anharmonic_K3_xi4.md` §2.4 corollary

- `543` 6 May Session 26 — Geometric core verification: any 2-plane through $c_i$ contains AT MOST 2 of the 4 LO face-normals of regular tetrahedron $\alpha_i$. Proof: any 3 of the 4 face-normals span $\mathbb{R}^3$ (they form 3 of 4 vertices of a non-degenerate regular tetrahedron centered at $c_i$). Hence no 2-plane contains 3 or more. By C2 (face-to-face contact requires centroid direction parallel to a face-normal), at most 2 face-coincident contacts have partners in $P$, so $\deg_G(c_i) \leq 2$ in coplanar contact graph → patch 0230

Step 4: strict edge gain $|E_{\mathrm{3D}}| - |E_{\mathrm{planar}}| \geq 2N_\alpha - 6 \geq 2$ at $N_\alpha \geq 4$, binding gain $\geq 2 B_{\mathrm{pair}} = 4.684$ MeV; by Lemma C + C5, no coplanar configuration is ground state → patch 0230

- `560` 6 May Session 27 — Sub-task (c) planning: C5 well-definedness via compactness. The §9 v0.1 Gap 1 entry already specifies the closure route ("compactness argument: all rigid-packing-compatible configurations at fixed $\Nalpha$ form a compact configuration space, so minima exist"). Insertion as new §2.7 between §2.6 (Sub-Lemma 2.2) and existing §2.7 (auxiliary assumptions, renumbered §2.8) → patch 0235
- `561` 6 May Session 27 — Configuration space definition: $\mathrm{Conf}(\Nalpha) = \{(c_i, R_i)_{i=1}^{\Nalpha} : \text{rigid packing}, |E| \geq \Nalpha - 1\} / \mathrm{SE}(3)$. Modulo rigid motions; restricted to $G$-connected (per C5's own physical-realizability requirement). $G$-connectedness equivalent to $|E| \geq \Nalpha - 1$ (spanning tree minimum) → patch 0235
- `562` 6 May Session 27 — Subtle point: the upper-semi-continuity argument requires careful handling. Face-coincidence is an EQUALITY condition (parallel face normals + centroid distance $\Raa$ + in-face vertex correspondence), defining CLOSED subvarieties. Indicator of closed set is USC. Sum of USC functions is USC. Hence $|E|$ and $B$ are USC. Generic perturbations BREAK contacts, so $|E|$ can drop along a perturbation path; USC means the LIMIT of $|E|$ is at most the value at the limit configuration → patch 0235
- `563` 6 May Session 27 — Proof structure: 5 steps. Step 1: $G$-connectedness gives diameter bound $(\Nalpha-1)\Raa$. Step 2: pre-compactness via embedding into compact product. Step 3: USC of $B$ via closed face-coincidence subvarieties. Step 4: attainment of supremum via maximizing-sequence + USC argument. Step 5: $\mathcal{C}^*$ interior to $\mathrm{Conf}(\Nalpha)$ via linear-chain floor → patch 0235
- `564` 6 May Session 27 — Key technical detail in Step 5: linear-chain configuration is feasible (rigid packing satisfied, $G$-connected via chain), gives $B = \Nalpha\Balpha + (\Nalpha-1)\Bpair$. So $\sup B \geq$ this floor, forcing $|E(\mathcal{C}^*)| \geq \Nalpha - 1$ since $\Bpair > 0$. This places $\mathcal{C}^*$ interior to original $\mathrm{Conf}$, not just on its closure boundary where $|E|$ might drop → patch 0235
